BASIL VALENTINE
HIS

— Triumphant Chariot
ANTIMONY.

Ince 1, Bajil Valentine, by Religious Vows ant
bound to live according to the Order of St.

| Benediit, and that requires another manner
of Spirit of Holinefs , then the common

tate of Mortals exercifed in the prophane buf
nefsof this World, I thought it my duty before

| all things, in the beginning of this hittle Book, to
yw) declare what isneceflary to be known by the pious
viii! Spag yri/f, inflamed with an ardent defire of this Arty
if!) as, what he ought to do,and whereunto to direét his
“4 aim, that he may lay fuch Foundations of the whole

| Matter, as may be ftable; left his Building fhaken
| with Winds, happen to fail, and the whole Edifice
| to be involved in fhameful Ruine, which other-

| wife, being founded on more firmand folid Princi-
| ples, might have continued for a long feries of
time. Which Admonition I judged was, is, and
always will be a neceflary part of my Religious
Office; efpecially, fince we mutt all die , and no one
of us, which now are, whether high or low, fhall
B long

nite

long be feen among the number of Men. — For it

Majefty, butalfo.that Men may obey him fincerely
in all tiNAgsS.

In this my Meditation I. found that there were
five € pric ipal Heads, chiefly to be confidered by the
wife and prudent Spectators of our Wifdom and
Art The firft of which is , Invocation of GOD.

True Preparation. The fourth, The Way of Ufing.
The fifth, Urility and Fruit. For he , who regards
not thefe, fall never obtain place among true
Chym ifts, or fill upthe number of perfect Spag ag yrifts.
There efore touching thefefive Heads we {hail here
follow! ingt treat, and fo far declare them, as thatthe
eeneral Work maybe brought to light and pet-
fected by an intent and ftudious Operator.

1. Invocation of GOD mult be made with a oo
fain Heavenly Intention, drawn from the botto
of apure and fincere Heart, and Confcience , free
from all Ambition, Hypocrifie, and all other Vices,
which have any affinity with thefe, as Arrogancy;
Bolduefs, Pride, Luxury, Mundane Petulancy , Op-

preflionof the Poor , and other dependent evils; '
al which are to be eradicated out of the Heart;

i when a Man defires to proftrate himfelt before
Throne of Grace, for obtaining bodily health,
emay do that with a Conftience free from all un-
pr sre table Weeds, that his Body wa be tranfmt i
into an Holy Temple of GOD, and be purged fro
every uncleannefs, For GOD will not be mock ced
